CONTROLLED EXPERIMENT COMPARISON
Question: F1 vs F2: performance / selection fairness / completion fairness
Status: COMPLETED
Changed factor: selection
Matrix config: D:\research\federated-system\configurations\fuzzy-matrix.json
Training/selection seeds: [42, 43, 44, 45, 46]
Dataset partitions are fixed across these seeds; dataset seed is stored in each manifest.
Ratios use scale 0..1. Differences below are RIGHT minus LEFT.
SD is sample standard deviation across seeds (n-1), not a confidence interval.
Wall seconds = sum of round wall times, including test evaluation, excluding initialization.
Virtual seconds include proxy wall time + weight calculation + aggregation, exclude observer test evaluation.
Communication cost = NOT_MODELED. Logical payload counts measure dense tensor bytes, not WAN traffic/latency.
RAM-feasible coverage uses a static common RAM denominator; estimated-feasible coverage also uses availability/deadline estimates and can be policy-dependent.
Shared traces are indexed by round/client, not by absolute virtual time. Job durations/deadline outcomes may differ.
Selector overhead: shared feasibility diagnostics + policy decision, measured consistently for every policy. Fairness bookkeeping is measured separately.
virtualSeconds excludes selector/bookkeeping; virtualSecondsWithSelector includes both. N/A means unavailable legacy metric or empty denominator.
Selection/completion Gini are population metrics; RAM/feasible-opportunity metrics exclude permanently impossible clients. Candidate sets may diverge with policy-specific timing histories.

Training/selection seeds: [42, 43, 44, 45, 46]
Dataset partitions are fixed across these seeds; dataset seed is stored in each manifest.
Ratios use scale 0..1. Differences below are RIGHT minus LEFT.
SD is sample standard deviation across seeds (n-1), not a confidence interval.
Wall seconds = sum of round wall times, including test evaluation, excluding initialization.
Virtual seconds include proxy wall time + weight calculation + aggregation, exclude observer test evaluation.
Communication cost = NOT_MODELED. Logical payload counts measure dense tensor bytes, not WAN traffic/latency.
RAM-feasible coverage uses a static common RAM denominator; estimated-feasible coverage also uses availability/deadline estimates and can be policy-dependent.
Shared traces are indexed by round/client, not by absolute virtual time. Job durations/deadline outcomes may differ.
Selector overhead: shared feasibility diagnostics + policy decision, measured consistently for every policy. Fairness bookkeeping is measured separately.
virtualSeconds excludes selector/bookkeeping; virtualSecondsWithSelector includes both. N/A means unavailable legacy metric or empty denominator.
Selection/completion Gini are population metrics; RAM/feasible-opportunity metrics exclude permanently impossible clients. Candidate sets may diverge with policy-specific timing histories.

Training/selection seeds: [42, 43, 44, 45, 46]
Dataset partitions are fixed across these seeds; dataset seed is stored in each manifest.
Ratios use scale 0..1. Differences below are RIGHT minus LEFT.
SD is sample standard deviation across seeds (n-1), not a confidence interval.
Wall seconds = sum of round wall times, including test evaluation, excluding initialization.
Virtual seconds include proxy wall time + weight calculation + aggregation, exclude observer test evaluation.
Communication cost = NOT_MODELED. Logical payload counts measure dense tensor bytes, not WAN traffic/latency.
RAM-feasible coverage uses a static common RAM denominator; estimated-feasible coverage also uses availability/deadline estimates and can be policy-dependent.
Shared traces are indexed by round/client, not by absolute virtual time. Job durations/deadline outcomes may differ.
Selector overhead: shared feasibility diagnostics + policy decision, measured consistently for every policy. Fairness bookkeeping is measured separately.
virtualSeconds excludes selector/bookkeeping; virtualSecondsWithSelector includes both. N/A means unavailable legacy metric or empty denominator.
Selection/completion Gini are population metrics; RAM/feasible-opportunity metrics exclude permanently impossible clients. Candidate sets may diverge with policy-specific timing histories.

Training/selection seeds: [42, 43, 44, 45, 46]
Dataset partitions are fixed across these seeds; dataset seed is stored in each manifest.
Ratios use scale 0..1. Differences below are RIGHT minus LEFT.
SD is sample standard deviation across seeds (n-1), not a confidence interval.
Wall seconds = sum of round wall times, including test evaluation, excluding initialization.
Virtual seconds include proxy wall time + weight calculation + aggregation, exclude observer test evaluation.
Communication cost = NOT_MODELED. Logical payload counts measure dense tensor bytes, not WAN traffic/latency.
RAM-feasible coverage uses a static common RAM denominator; estimated-feasible coverage also uses availability/deadline estimates and can be policy-dependent.
Shared traces are indexed by round/client, not by absolute virtual time. Job durations/deadline outcomes may differ.
Selector overhead: shared feasibility diagnostics + policy decision, measured consistently for every policy. Fairness bookkeeping is measured separately.
virtualSeconds excludes selector/bookkeeping; virtualSecondsWithSelector includes both. N/A means unavailable legacy metric or empty denominator.
Selection/completion Gini are population metrics; RAM/feasible-opportunity metrics exclude permanently impossible clients. Candidate sets may diverge with policy-specific timing histories.

Ratios use scale 0..1. Differences below are RIGHT minus LEFT.
SD is sample standard deviation across seeds (n-1), not a confidence interval.
Wall seconds = sum of round wall times, including test evaluation, excluding initialization.
Virtual seconds include proxy wall time + weight calculation + aggregation, exclude observer test evaluation.
Communication cost = NOT_MODELED. Logical payload counts measure dense tensor bytes, not WAN traffic/latency.
RAM-feasible coverage uses a static common RAM denominator; estimated-feasible coverage also uses availability/deadline estimates and can be policy-dependent.
Shared traces are indexed by round/client, not by absolute virtual time. Job durations/deadline outcomes may differ.
Selector overhead: shared feasibility diagnostics + policy decision, measured consistently for every policy. Fairness bookkeeping is measured separately.
virtualSeconds excludes selector/bookkeeping; virtualSecondsWithSelector includes both. N/A means unavailable legacy metric or empty denominator.
Selection/completion Gini are population metrics; RAM/feasible-opportunity metrics exclude permanently impossible clients. Candidate sets may diverge with policy-specific timing histories.

Ratios use scale 0..1. Differences below are RIGHT minus LEFT.
SD is sample standard deviation across seeds (n-1), not a confidence interval.
Wall seconds = sum of round wall times, including test evaluation, excluding initialization.
Virtual seconds include proxy wall time + weight calculation + aggregation, exclude observer test evaluation.
Communication cost = NOT_MODELED. Logical payload counts measure dense tensor bytes, not WAN traffic/latency.
RAM-feasible coverage uses a static common RAM denominator; estimated-feasible coverage also uses availability/deadline estimates and can be policy-dependent.
Shared traces are indexed by round/client, not by absolute virtual time. Job durations/deadline outcomes may differ.
Selector overhead: shared feasibility diagnostics + policy decision, measured consistently for every policy. Fairness bookkeeping is measured separately.
virtualSeconds excludes selector/bookkeeping; virtualSecondsWithSelector includes both. N/A means unavailable legacy metric or empty denominator.
Selection/completion Gini are population metrics; RAM/feasible-opportunity metrics exclude permanently impossible clients. Candidate sets may diverge with policy-specific timing histories.
